# CHANGELOG

## Maintenance Principles

### 日本語

- 本ファイルは `README.md` と同様に、日本語、英語、中国語を同時に維持します。
- 説明と作業記録は、日本語を先に置き、次に英語、その後に中国語を置きます。
- 重要な作業ごとに、短い要約だけでなく詳細なタイムラインを残します。
- 各記録には、可能な限り JST 時刻、背景、影響範囲、主要ファイルまたはディレクトリ、実行手順、検証結果、残タスクを含めます。
- 本リポジトリ本文には公開インターネット情報、公的資料、公開開示、または公開情報に基づく分析のみを残します。

### English

- This file, like `README.md`, is maintained in Japanese, English, and Chinese together.
- Explanatory text and work records put Japanese first, English second, and Chinese third.
- Important work must leave a detailed timeline rather than only a short summary.
- Each record should include JST time, background, scope, main files or directories, execution steps, validation results, and follow-up items whenever possible.
- Body content in this repository is limited to public internet information, official materials, public disclosures, or analysis based on public sources.

### 中文

- 本文件与 `README.md` 一样，必须同时维护日文、英文和中文。
- 说明与工作记录必须按日文在前、英文在中、中文在后的顺序排列。
- 重要工作必须保留详细时间线，不能只写简短概述。
- 每条记录应尽可能包含 JST 时间、背景、范围、主要文件或目录、执行步骤、验证结果与后续事项。
- 本仓库正文只允许使用互联网公开信息、官方资料、公开披露或基于公开来源的分析。

## 2026-07-29 - Table provenance batch 05 corrections (#235)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-29 16:27:38 JST / 背景:** Issue #235 の historical snapshot は 135 warnings / 64 paths だったが、released-main `c22ee9c8` で同じ path ownership を再監査すると 132 warnings だった。内訳は `card-issuers/` 40 / 23 paths、`consumer-finance/` 13 / 5、`leasing-firms/` 35 / 21、`loyalty/` 44 / 15。目的は marker の機械挿入ではなく、owned tables / rows と直近 context の public-primary-source revalidation である。
- **範囲 / 主要ファイル:** 上記 4 domains の 64 source entries、`site/src/content/i18n/{ja,en}/` の 128 mirrors、三言語 `README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/{README,v2026.07.29-14}.md`、root `index.html`、AI discovery / API surfaces。`.opinions/**`、`docs/**`、人手判断を待つ Issue #193、後続 #236–#244 は変更しない。
- **実行手順 / source correction:** 3 つの disjoint writer packet と独立 semantic QA を並行し、generic homepage laundering、dead URL、法人誤認、設立年・株主・登録の誤り、未開示 KPI、accounting / settlement overclaim を返修した。ACOM の MUFG 連結・株主持分、Aiful / JCB / Loyalty Marketing の主要株主、JCB の Amex / 中国銀聯年表、北海道リース・NEC・東京センチュリー・浜銀ファイナンスの法人境界、2020 Ponta 移行、d Point rank、V Point ID 連携を公式資料へ揃えた。実在を確認できない Dentsu finance entity、JR 東日本 payment subsidiary / Suica Pay、2024 Ponta 統合、未開示 liability migration / bilateral settlement / entity-specific accounting を確定事実から除いた。
- **検証 / mirror・残タスク:** source-level `provenance:audit` は full 1,723 → 1,591、Issue #235 owned 132 → 0、残り 1,524 table-level + 67 mixed-row。semantic QA 後の stale claim / dead-route pattern scan と `git diff --check` は clean。128 mirrors は source body 64 / 64 exact、英語 language / parity issues 0、`i18n:check` は ja / en 各 1,442 current、stale / orphaned / missing 0、`fidelity: ok` 各 1,442。`audit:all --as-of 2026-07-29` は factual conflict / provenance threshold / Tier-1 due 0（queue monitor 1 は advisory）、`wiki:audit:ci` は 1,489 entries / issues 0 / drift 0。`release:write` は Markdown 1,604 / 40 domains / 1,489 entries / 11,001,092 chars / 1,772,025 tokens と 1,603 public pages / 1,482 sitemap・API entries を同期した。full `bun run verify --out _site` は Bun 1.3.14、dependency vulnerabilities 0、Astro diagnostics 0、98 / 98 tests、2,969 built pages、2,968 Pagefind pages、284,480 hrefs / 5,864 routes、6,054 generated URLs、`git diff --check` を PASS。残タスクは real pre-push、`pre` / `main` PR、production deployment、same-SHA `v2026.07.29-14`、#235 close と親 #192 / #236 更新である。

- **Scope / Primary files:** The 64 source entries in those four domains; 128 mirrors under `site/src/content/i18n/{ja,en}/`; trilingual `README.md`; this CHANGELOG; `releases/{README,v2026.07.29-14}.md`; root `index.html`; and AI discovery / API surfaces. `.opinions/**`, `docs/**`, human-review Issue #193, and downstream Issues #236–#244 remain unchanged.

Full `bun run verify --out _site` passed on Bun 1.3.14 with zero dependency vulnerabilities, zero Astro diagnostics, 98 / 98 tests, 2,969 built pages, 2,968 Pagefind pages, 284,480 hrefs / 5,864 routes, 6,054 generated URLs, and a clean `git diff --check`. Remaining work is the real pre-push, `pre` / `main` PRs, production deployment, same-SHA `v2026.07.29-14`, closing #235, and updating parent #192 / #236.

## 2026-07-29 - Table provenance batch 04 corrections (#234)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-29 14:45:49 JST / 背景:** Issue #234 の current released-main `f3e78cca` baseline は、`business/` 60 warnings / 16 paths、`corporate-strategy/` 35 / 10、`retail/` 19 / 6、合計 114 `table_without_provenance` warnings / 32 source paths だった。目的は marker の機械挿入ではなく、企業・規制当局・取引所の公式資料、法令、発行会社・取引当事者の公表資料を用いて owned table と直近 evidence context を再検証することだった。
- **範囲 / 主要ファイル:** 上記 3 domains の 32 source entries、`site/src/content/i18n/{ja,en}/` の 64 mirrors、三言語 `README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/{README,v2026.07.29-13}.md`、root `index.html`、AI discovery / API surfaces。`.opinions/**`、`docs/**`、人手判断を待つ Issue #193、後続 #235–#244 は変更しない。9 entries の metadata を 2026-07-29 / review 2027-01-29 へ更新し、他の対象 entry は現在の risk cadence を維持した。
- **実行手順 / content:** Coinbase の直接上場、CZ / Binance の刑事・民事手続、Arm / PayPay の上場、Sony FG の部分スピンオフ、東芝 TOB を、完了・予定・条件付き状態に分離した。みずほ / 楽天、Seven Bank / Seven & i / 伊藤忠、FamilyMart ATM、Lawson の 50/50 所有について、法人、議決権、会計上の支配、持分法、販売チャネルを区別した。会社分割、株式交換・移転、株式交付・分配、合併、持株会社化、グループ通算では、会社法上の仕組みと税務上の適格 / 非適格判定を分離し、単一比率・対価による断定を撤回した。
- **実行手順 / mirrors・検証:** 64 mirrors を source-language exact sync または guarded full sync + manual review で更新した。candidate audit は source hash、英語 body 完全一致、heading / table / list / blockquote shape、wikilink target、URL、provenance marker、日付・金額・比率、否定・条件表現を確認し blocking problems 0。`i18n:status` は ja / en 各 1,442 current、stale / orphaned / missing 0、`fidelity: ok` 各 1,442。`provenance:audit` は full 1,837 → 1,723、Issue #234 owned 114 → 0、残り 1,645 table-level + 78 mixed-row。`audit:all --as-of 2026-07-29`、`wiki:audit:ci`、`release:write`、full `bun run verify --out _site`、`git diff --check` を PASS。残タスクは real pre-push、`pre` / `main` PR checks、production deployment、同一 main SHA の `v2026.07.29-13`、#234 close と親 #192 / residual queue 更新である。

## 2026-07-29 - Table provenance batch 03 corrections (#233)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-29 12:43:09 JST / 背景:** Issue #233 の current released-main `70cc5493` baseline は `table_without_provenance` / `table_row_without_marker` 146 warnings / 69 source paths だった。内訳は `banking/` 103 / 31 paths、`cooperative-banks/` 43 / 38 paths。目的は warning 数を marker で機械的に下げることではなく、法令、金融庁・日銀資料、預金保険制度、法人・業界団体の公式概要・沿革・開示により、owned table / row と直近 evidence context を再検証することだった。
- **範囲 / 主要ファイル:** 上記 2 domain の 69 source entries、`site/src/content/i18n/{ja,en}/` の 138 mirrors、三言語 `README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/{README,v2026.07.29-12}.md`、root `index.html`、AI discovery / API surfaces。`.opinions/**`、`docs/**`、人手判断を待つ Issue #193、後続 #234–#244 は変更しない。source metadata は 2026-07-29、review cadence は各 entry の current risk に合わせた。
- **実行手順 / content・mirror:** table 全体を同じ source set が支える場合だけ scoped lead / footer marker を使い、根拠が行ごとに異なる場合は row-level marker / direct public link を維持した。banking lane の免許、法令、監督、預金保険、日銀取引経路と、cooperative-banks lane の現法人、合併・前身、中央機関、業界団体、共同 IT、運用会社を法人単位で校正した。しんきん共同センターの架空会社説明、名古屋 / 北九州 / 札幌の entity misidentification、農林中央金庫の旧本店・旧法・「特殊法人」「中央銀行」「最後の貸し手」・規模順位を撤回した。138 mirrors は source-language exact sync または guarded line-level sync + manual review で更新し、hash、Markdown shape、link target、URL、marker、金額・比率を照合した。旧 mirror の `¥10 million → 10 万円`、`¥2 billion → 2 億円` 等の数量級誤訳も修正した。
- **検証結果 / 残タスク:** candidate audit は 138 files / blocking problems 0。`i18n:status` は ja / en 各 1,442 current、stale / orphaned / missing 0、`fidelity: ok` 各 1,442。`provenance:audit` は full 1,983 → 1,837、Issue #233 owned 146 → 0、両 domain owned residual 0、残り 1,759 table-level + 78 mixed-row。`audit:all --as-of 2026-07-29`、`wiki:audit:ci`、`release:write`、full `bun run verify --out _site`、`git diff --check` を PASS。残タスクは real pre-push、`pre` / `main` PR checks、production deployment、同一 main SHA の `v2026.07.29-12`、#233 close と親 #192 / 残存 queue 更新である。

- **2026-07-29 12:43:09 JST / Background:** Issue #233's current released-main `70cc5493` baseline contained 146 `table_without_provenance` / `table_row_without_marker` warnings across 69 source paths: 103 / 31 paths under `banking/` and 43 / 38 under `cooperative-banks/`. The objective was not to lower the warning count through mechanical marker insertion, but to revalidate each owned table / row and its immediate evidence context against statutes, FSA and Bank of Japan material, deposit-insurance systems, and official entity / industry-body profiles, histories, and disclosures.

### 日本語

- **範囲 / 主要ファイル:** `regional-banks/{daiwa-next-bank,iyogin-hd,nanto-bank,okinawa-fg,ryukyu-bank,ssnb}.md`、`retail/japan-retail-financial-distribution-wedge-matrix.md`、`securities-firms/{aizawa-securities,iwai-cosmo-securities,monex-group,mufg-securities,smbc-nikko,tokai-tokyo-securities}.md`、ja / en 26 mirrors、三言語 `README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/{README,v2026.07.29-9}.md`、root index / discovery surfaces。`.opinions/**`、`docs/**`、Issue #231 の table-related residual queue は変更しない。

## 2026-07-29 - Fintech, insurance, leasing, life, and PayPay low-marker corrections (#222)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-29 04:26:01 JST / 背景:** Issue #221 / #247 の release 後、Issue #222 が所有する current released-main scope は fintech、外国金融機関、insurance、leasing、life insurers、loyalty、megabanks の 11 paths / 13 `low_marker_density` warnings だった。目的は marker の機械挿入ではなく、公式一次資料で主張を再確認し、未開示の推計、実現していない制度・統合、古い ownership / listing status を確定事実から除外することである。
- **範囲 / 主要ファイル:** `fintech/{paypal-pyusd-stablecoin,three-circles-mra-2030-economic-scale,wholesale-settlement-network-matrix}.md`、`foreign-financial-institutions/foreign-bank-branches-japan-index.md`、`insurance/sony-life-group-life-operating-model.md`、`leasing-firms/toyota-financial.md`、`life-insurers/{dai-ichi-life,meiji-yasuda}.md`、`loyalty/sb-yahoo-paypay-points-unified-ecosystem.md`、`megabanks/{japan-post-holdings,paypay-fg}.md` と ja / en mirrors。factual-consistency audit が Toyota 設立日の duplicate-route conflict を検出したため、`manufacturer-finance/toyota-financial-services.md` と 2 mirrors も同じ official profile に整合した。三言語 `README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/{README,v2026.07.29-7}.md`、root index / discovery surfaces を同じ release unit に含める。`.opinions/**` と `docs/**` は変更しない。
- **実行手順 / fintech・制度:** PayPal official material で PYUSD の launch、Solana、supported networks、70-market expansion を確認し、reserve split、chain share、DEX liquidity 等の未確認値を除外した。GENIUS Act を S.1582 / Public Law 119-27 へ訂正し、`$130B` / `$384B` を scenario assumptions として隔離した。FSA-SEC dialogue / minister remarks は国際協調の議論であって signed MRA ではない。Fnality、Partior、Kinexys、mBridge、Agorá、Mariana は official status / currencies / volume / handover に沿って production、MVP、prototype、proof of concept を分離した。

### English

- **2026-07-29 03:21:06 JST / Background:** After Issue #219 calibrated the table-aware detector, the non-table `low_marker_density` queue contained 56 warnings. Issue #221 owns a bounded content review of 17 of those warnings across 15 source entries in agent, banking, business, exchanges, and financial-licence domains. The objective is not mechanical marker insertion; it is to recheck nearby claims, figures, legal entities and parents, membership/licence status, and regulatory transition dates against official primary sources. The first full verify exposed a translation-protection bug: URL-bearing provenance markers were whole-marker masked after their URLs had already been masked, creating nested placeholders that one-pass `unmask` could not restore. After confirming there was no duplicate issue, Issue #247 was opened and repaired as a blocker in the same release.
- **Scope / Primary files:** `agent-economy/privy-embedded-wallet-overview.md`; `banking/{custody-bank-operating-model,japan-foreign-bank-retreat-refocus-timeline-matrix,japan-stablecoin-bank-perimeter-2025,sony-bank}.md`; `business/larry-fink-blackrock-digital-asset-template.md`; `exchanges/{btc-spot-etf-japan-impact,japan-cex-parent-fg-adjacency-matrix,japan-vasp-business-model-competitive-matrix,jp-exchange-bitflyer,jp-exchange-dmm-com-securities,jp-exchange-sblox,jp-vasp-parent-company-map,uk-fca-crypto-registration-overview}.md`; `financial-licenses/securities-license-stack.md`; the 30 corresponding `site/src/content/i18n/{ja,en}/**` mirrors; `site/scripts/{protect.mjs,protect.test.mjs}`; `docs/05-functional-specs/i18n-pipeline.md`; `docs/07-quality/test-plan.md`; trilingual `README.md`, this CHANGELOG, and `releases/{README,v2026.07.29-6}.md`; plus root-index and discovery surfaces. `.opinions/**` and residual queues owned by other issues remain unchanged.
- **Steps / Registry and regime corrections:** Checked FSA workbooks on 2026-07-29 and recorded 57 foreign-bank branches, 26 crypto-asset exchange providers, one electronic-payment-instrument service provider whose handled-instrument field lists USDC / RLUSD / JPYSC, and 1,954 financial instruments business operators, each with a scoped marker. The FSA FAQ confirms that a category-3 trust-beneficiary-right EPI may be issued by a trust company or authorised trust financial institution, not only a trust bank. FCA material now separates current MLR registration from the 2026-09-30 to 2027-02-28 application window and the new FSMA regime commencing on 2027-10-25.
- **Steps / Entity and claim corrections:** Used the 75M+ accounts / 1,000+ developer teams disclosed in Privy's official 2025-06-11 announcement and removed the undisclosed acquisition price, 110M-wallet figure, and valuation. Official Laser Digital, JVCEA, Crypto Garage, and FSA sources show that Laser Digital Japan is a JVCEA 第二種会員 rather than a Type II Financial Instruments Business registrant, while Crypto Garage is the Digital Garage + Tokyo Tanshi joint venture and crypto-asset exchange provider No. 00029; the unsupported Daiwa parent / in-group bank route was removed. The old bitFlyer private cap table, unverified Sony FG opening price, unproven BlackRock / SEC ETP AUM and Japan-market causation, and CBJ custody-majority / merger-cost-allocation claims were limited to what primary sources establish. DMM.com Securities was separated from DMM Bitcoin as a legal entity and registrant, and S.BLOX's company rename was separated from its service rename.
- **Translation / metadata synchronization:** Updated `last_updated` / `last_tended` to 2026-07-29 and `review_by` to 2026-10-27 across all 15 source entries. Propagated the scoped markers and official links to the ja/en mirrors, then refreshed all 30 `source_hash` and `translated_at` fields under the source-body SHA-256 contract. Issue #247 now atomically masks a complete `^[...]` marker before URLs, dates, and numbers, preventing nested placeholders inside the marker. Added single- and multi-URL fixtures and documented the lossless one-pass round-trip contract in the functional specification and test plan. `bun run i18n:status -- --json` reports 1,442 mirrors per language, all current, with zero stale, orphaned, missing, or source-pointer-drift rows and 1,442 `fidelity: ok` mirrors per language.
- **Validation:** Focused `bun run provenance:audit -- --json` moved full warnings from 2,336 to 2,319, `low_marker_density` from 56 to 39, Issue #221's owned rows from 17 to zero, and retained `needs_review=0`; the 2,280 table-related warnings are unchanged. `bun test site/scripts/protect.test.mjs` passed 13 tests / 51 assertions. `bun run audit:all -- --as-of 2026-07-29` completed with zero blocking rows, and `bun run wiki:audit:ci` reported 1,489 entries / zero issues / zero canonical drift. The first full verify correctly blocked the bug. - **Known notes / Follow-up:** The provenance report remains advisory; the remaining 39 low-marker and 2,280 table-related warnings are human-review queues owned by separate bounded issues. ## 2026-07-29 - Table-level provenance proximity calibration (#219)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-29 02:40:40 JST / 背景:** 親 Issue #192 が #219 に割り当てた snapshot は、reason が exactly `table_row_without_marker` の advisory warning 6,311 rows / 1,089 paths / 39 domains だった。旧 detector は factual-looking な table row ごとに warning を出し、同じ table の scoped heading marker、lead / caption、footer、section source block、または row 内 direct public link を解釈しなかったため、content review 前に detector precision を確立する必要があった。#220 後の exact release base では同 reason が 6,313 rows になった。
- **範囲 / 主要ファイル:** `tools/provenance_completeness_audit.ts` と focused test、`docs/07-quality/{provenance-source-completeness-audit,test-plan}.md`、三言語 `README.md` / 本 CHANGELOG / `releases/{README,v2026.07.29-5}.md`、release generator が同期する public surfaces。wiki corpus 本文、ja / en mirrors、`.opinions/**`、#221-#224 の non-table low-marker queue は変更しない。
- **実行手順 / detector contract:** Markdown table を structural header、factual rows、same-section context として group 化した。row 自身の `^[marker]` / direct public HTTP(S) link、marker / link を持つ直前 section heading、明示的に table scope を示す lead / caption、marker-bearing table header、source-labelled footer / section block を証拠として区別する。section source proximity は最大 3 logical blocks / 12 physical lines とし、generic nearby marker、隣接 row marker、遠い `## Sources` は cover に使わない。完全未出典 table は 1 件の `table_without_provenance`、mixed table は未 cover row だけを `table_row_without_marker` とする。大きい JSON は awaited stdout write で完全出力する。
- **分類結果:** release-base 6,313 old rows のうち 5,656 rows は 2,175 table-level tasks へ統合、599 rows は scoped evidence により detector false positive、58 rows は row warning のまま維持した。隣接 marker spillover を廃止したことで 47 mixed-table rows が新たに可視化され、post-calibration queue は 2,175 table + 105 mixed-row = 2,280 warnings / 1,064 paths / 39 domains。non-table `low_marker_density` 56 を含む full report は warning 2,336 / `needs_review=0`。
- **代表 sample / routing:** marker-bearing heading は `megabanks/mufg.md`、direct row links は `financial-regulators/financial-reports-2020-index.md`、linked colon lead は `security/proxy-upgrade-rug-pattern.md`、mixed rows は `asset-managers/saison-asset-management.md`、homogeneous unsourced table は `banking/baas-japan-landscape.md` で確認した。39 domains の全 residual を first path component で disjoint な 14 child issues #231-#244 へ exact routing し、合計 2,280 warnings / 1,064 paths を一致させた。
- **検証結果:** focused provenance suite は 8 tests / 13 assertions PASS。`bun run provenance:audit -- --json | jq` は pipe buffer を超える complete JSON を parse し、table reasons 2,280、full warnings 2,336、`needs_review=0` を確認した。`bun run audit:all -- --as-of 2026-07-29` は blocking count 0。full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` も PASS し、release docs 24 files / 22 strict notes / 76 grandfathered notes、wiki 1,489 entries / issues 0 / canonical drift 0、generated API 1,482 / fixed-timestamp identity / leakage 0、ja / en mirrors 各 1,442 current、production vulnerability 0、Astro check 44 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints、97 tests / 323 assertions、Astro 2,969 pages、entry metadata 2,884 / issues 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro + 3,081 raw files、13 required routes、final HTML 2,969 files / 5,864 routes / 283,184 hrefs、generated URL 6,045、`git diff --check` を確認した。
- **既知の注意点 / 残タスク:** audit は read-only / advisory のままで marker 自動挿入や hard gate は追加しない。2,280 residuals は public source scope の human review queue であり、誤りの断定ではない。`bun run release:write`、full `bun run verify --out _site`、real pre-push、`pre` / `main` PR checks、production deployment を通し、同じ main merge SHA に `v2026.07.29-5` tag / GitHub Release を作る。Issue #219 と親 #192 に exact evidence を記録した後、#231 から content review を開始する。

### English

- **2026-07-29 02:40:40 JST / Background:** Parent Issue #192 assigned #219 a snapshot of 6,311 advisory warnings across 1,089 paths and 39 domains whose reason was exactly `table_row_without_marker`. The old detector warned on every factual-looking table row without interpreting a scoped heading marker, lead/caption, footer, section source block, or direct public link in the row, so detector precision had to be established before content review. The exact release base after #220 contained 6,313 rows with that reason.
- **Scope / Primary files:** `tools/provenance_completeness_audit.ts` and its focused test, `docs/07-quality/{provenance-source-completeness-audit,test-plan}.md`, trilingual `README.md`, this CHANGELOG, `releases/{README,v2026.07.29-5}.md`, and public surfaces synchronized by the release generator. Wiki corpus bodies, ja/en mirrors, `.opinions/**`, and the non-table low-marker queue in #221-#224 remain unchanged.
- **Steps / Detector contract:** Grouped each Markdown table into a structural header, factual rows, and same-section context. The detector now distinguishes row-level `^[marker]` / direct public HTTP(S) links; an immediately preceding section heading with a marker/link; an explicitly table-scoped lead/caption; a marker-bearing table header; and source-labelled footers/section blocks. Section-source proximity is capped at three logical blocks and twelve physical lines; generic nearby markers, adjacent-row markers, and distant `## Sources` do not cover the table. A wholly unsupported table emits one `table_without_provenance`, while a mixed table emits `table_row_without_marker` only for uncovered rows. Large JSON output now uses an awaited stdout write.
- **Classification result:** Of the 6,313 old release-base rows, 5,656 consolidate into 2,175 table-level tasks, 599 are scoped-evidence detector false positives, and 58 remain row warnings. Removing adjacent-marker spillover newly exposes 47 mixed-table rows, yielding a post-calibration queue of 2,175 table plus 105 mixed-row warnings = 2,280 warnings across 1,064 paths and 39 domains. Including 56 non-table `low_marker_density` rows, the full report is 2,336 warnings with `needs_review=0`.
- **Representative sampling / Routing:** Verified marker-bearing headings with `megabanks/mufg.md`, direct row links with `financial-regulators/financial-reports-2020-index.md`, a linked colon lead with `security/proxy-upgrade-rug-pattern.md`, mixed rows with `asset-managers/saison-asset-management.md`, and a homogeneous unsupported table with `banking/baas-japan-landscape.md`. Routed every residual in all 39 domains by disjoint first path component into 14 child issues #231-#244, reconciling exactly to 2,280 warnings / 1,064 paths.
- **Validation:** The focused provenance suite passed with 8 tests / 13 assertions. `bun run provenance:audit -- --json | jq` parsed complete JSON beyond the pipe-buffer size and confirmed 2,280 table-reason rows, 2,336 full warnings, and `needs_review=0`. `bun run audit:all -- --as-of 2026-07-29` completed with zero blocking rows. Full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` also passed: 24 release-doc files / 22 strict notes / 76 grandfathered notes; 1,489 wiki entries / zero issues / zero canonical drift; 1,482 generated API entries / fixed-timestamp identity / zero leakage; 1,442 current mirrors per language; zero production vulnerabilities; Astro check across 44 files with 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints; 97 tests / 323 assertions; 2,969 Astro pages; 2,884 entry-metadata pages with zero issues; 2,968 Pagefind pages; assembly of 6,258 Astro plus 3,081 raw files; all 13 required routes; 2,969 final HTML files / 5,864 routes / 283,184 hrefs; 6,045 unique generated URLs; and `git diff --check`.
- **Known notes / Follow-up:** The audit remains read-only and advisory, with no automatic marker insertion or hard gate. The 2,280 residuals are a human-review queue for public-source scope, not a declaration of errors. Run `bun run release:write`, full `bun run verify --out _site`, the real pre-push hook, both `pre` / `main` PR checks, and production deployment; create tag/GitHub Release `v2026.07.29-5` on the same main merge SHA. After recording exact evidence on #219 and parent #192, begin content review with #231.

## 2026-07-29 - Fact-freshness event inference calibration (#217)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-29 01:10:45 JST / 背景:** Issue #192 の 2026-07-29 queue census は freshness actionable 458 rows を報告し、そのうち 457 rows を `event`、455 rows を `cadence_due,event_keyword` と分類した。しかし 455 rows はすべて `days_overdue=0` で、明示的 `review_by` は 2026-11-15 から 2027-06-05 までの将来日だった。原因は route / title / tags と先頭 4,000 body characters を一つの broad regex に渡し、本文の “use case”、M&A / IPO の背景説明、frontmatter の `status:` key まで active 45-day event signal として扱ったことにある。
- **範囲:** fact-freshness class inference、reason-code contract、frontmatter / body boundary、focused fixtures、active architecture note、三言語 README、release registry、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.29-2.md` と release generator が同期する公開 discovery surface。wiki facts、source entry frontmatter、ja / en mirrors、hard release gate は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `tools/fact_freshness_audit.ts`、`tools/fact_freshness_audit.test.ts`、`docs/04-architecture/fact-freshness-source-recheck.md`、`README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/{README,v2026.07.29-2}.md`。
- **実行手順:** frontmatter を body context から除外した。45-day `event` は route / title に明示された lifecycle-state cue に限定し、transaction / bankruptcy / migration topic、topical tags と case study は `volatile_topic_keyword` / `case_study_keyword` として最大 90-day `high` に留めた。registry / statistics cue は本文 context を使えるが active event には昇格させない。generic “use case”、true acquisition deadline、historical case study、merger process、operator registry、future-reviewed ordinary profile を一つの deterministic fixture で固定した。
- **検証結果:** focused fixture は 1 test / 13 assertions、全 repository suite は 87 tests / 307 assertions で PASS。`docs:audit` と 67 active docs / 16 checks の `docs:stale` も PASS。exact 2026-07-29 audit は freshness actionable を 458 から 3 へ減らし、455 cadence-only rows は detector false positives として解消した。残る 3 rows は `agent-economy/{circle-agent-stack-usdc,coinbase-agentkit-agentic-wallets,google-a2a-agent-interop-protocol}.md` の `candidate_status` で、既に Issue #220 に全件割り当て済み。consistency 46 rows / 8 groups と provenance 6,366 warnings は本変更で隠さず、それぞれ #218 / #219 / #221-#224 の ownership を維持する。full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` も PASS し、release docs 21 files / 19 strict notes / 76 grandfathered notes、wiki 1,489 entries / issues 0 / canonical drift 0、generated API 1,482 entries / fixed-timestamp byte identity / leakage 0、ja / en 各 1,442 current mirrors、production vulnerability 0、Astro check 44 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints、Astro 2,969 pages、entry metadata 2,884 / issues 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,078 raw files、13 required routes、final HTML audit 5,864 routes、generated URL audit 6,042 unique internal URLs、`git diff --check` を確認した。
- **既知の注意点:** report は advisory のままで automatic content edit や hard release gate を追加しない。transaction topic を `high` とすることは public fact の currentness を証明せず、明示的 lifecycle state、candidate / borderline status、`review_by_due` は引き続き actionable である。Issue #193 の `.opinions/**` boundary には触れない。
- **残タスク:** current source commit で real pre-push を通し、`pre` / `main` PR checks、production deployment、same-SHA tag / GitHub Release `v2026.07.29-2` を完了する。Issue #217 に exact audit delta と release evidence を記録して close し、残る 3 candidate rows は #220 で public-source recheck する。

### English

- **2026-07-29 01:10:45 JST / Background:** Issue #192's 2026-07-29 queue census reported 458 actionable freshness rows, classifying 457 as `event` and 455 as `cadence_due,event_keyword`. All 455 had `days_overdue=0`, however, with explicit future `review_by` dates from 2026-11-15 through 2027-06-05. The cause was one broad regex over route/title/tags plus the first 4,000 body characters, which treated body “use case” phrases, contextual M&A/IPO discussion, and even the frontmatter `status:` key as active 45-day event signals.
- **Scope:** Fact-freshness class inference, the reason-code contract, the frontmatter/body boundary, focused fixtures, the active architecture note, the trilingual README, release registry, this CHANGELOG, `releases/v2026.07.29-2.md`, and public discovery surfaces synchronized by the release generator. Wiki facts, source-entry frontmatter, ja/en mirrors, and the hard release gate are unchanged.
- **Primary files:** `tools/fact_freshness_audit.ts`, `tools/fact_freshness_audit.test.ts`, `docs/04-architecture/fact-freshness-source-recheck.md`, `README.md`, this CHANGELOG, and `releases/{README,v2026.07.29-2}.md`.
- **Steps:** Removed frontmatter from body context. Limited the 45-day `event` class to explicit lifecycle-state cues in the route/title; transaction, bankruptcy, and migration topics, topical tags, and case studies now remain at most 90-day `high` with `volatile_topic_keyword` / `case_study_keyword`. Registry/statistics cues may still use body context but cannot promote a page to an active event. Fixed one deterministic fixture covering a generic “use case,” a true acquisition deadline, a historical case study, a merger process, an operator registry, and an ordinary profile with a future review date.
- **Validation:** The focused fixture passed 1 test / 13 assertions, and the complete repository suite passed 87 tests / 307 assertions. `docs:audit` and `docs:stale` across 67 active docs / 16 checks also passed. The exact 2026-07-29 audit reduced actionable freshness from 458 to 3; all 455 cadence-only rows were resolved as detector false positives. ## 2026-07-29 - Entry-discovery build graph and cache invalidation repair (#195)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-29 00:42:43 JST / 背景:** Issue #195。article-end discovery は page ごとに `getCollection('entries')` を呼び、約 1,482 routes を map / sort して `count:first:last` cache signature を再計算していた。初回以後の index map は再利用されても全 collection の signature sort と route lane 計算は 2,884 localized entry pages で反復され、中間 entry の title、tags、`related` link change は cache を失効させなかった。
- **範囲:** entry-discovery fingerprint / route graph、entry static-path integration、EntryLayout collection boundary、focused regression tests、architecture / FSD / RTM、三言語 README、release registry、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.29.md`。wiki facts、`ja` / `en` translation bodies、card markup / CSS / limits、wikilink / backlink semantics は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `site/src/lib/entryDiscovery.mjs`、`site/src/pages/[lang]/[...slug].astro`、`site/src/layouts/EntryLayout.astro`、`tools/entry_discovery.test.ts`、`docs/04-architecture/entry-discovery-build-graph.md`、`docs/{README,03-requirements/rtm,04-architecture/astro-site-architecture,05-functional-specs/ui-ux}.md`、`README.md`、`releases/{README,v2026.07.29}.md`。
- **実行手順:** 実装前に design packet を Issue と tracked architecture note に固定した。全 entry の normalized route / domain / slug / title / sorted tags / ordered `related` target / loader body を length-prefix して SHA-256 fingerprint を作り、旧 signature を置換した。canonical collection から route-only lanes を entry `getStaticPaths()` で一度だけ構築し、各 lang / route の localized payload を static props で渡した。`EntryLayout` から collection load と full-corpus scan を除去し、explicit related → backlinks → shared tags → read-next → sibling fallback と 5 / 3 / 5 caps を維持した。
- **検証結果:** focused cache/parity suite は 4 tests / 22 assertions、全 repository suite は 86 tests / 294 assertions、Astro check は 44 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints、duplicate-ID audit は 2,969 pages / issues 0 で PASS。同じ warmed content-cache / empty-`dist` 条件の build は旧 51.21 秒から新 21.18 秒へ 30.03 秒（58.6%）短縮し、static-route phase は 37.42 秒から 7.78 秒へ 79.2% 短縮した。変更前後の 2,884 entry discovery fragments は byte-identical / differences 0。full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` も、release docs 20 files / 18 strict notes / 76 grandfathered notes、wiki 1,489 entries / issues 0 / canonical drift 0、generated API 1,482 entries / fixed-timestamp byte identity / leakage 0、ja / en 各 1,442 current mirrors、production vulnerability 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,077 raw files、13 required routes、final HTML audit 5,864 routes、generated URL audit 6,041 unique internal URLs、`git diff --check` を含む全 gate で PASS した。
- **既知の注意点:** cache は process-local で persistent build artifact を作らない。backlink index は resolved wikilink から build process 内で一度構築する。`.opinions/**` の public boundary は Issue #193 の human decision 待ちであり、本変更は触れない。
- **残タスク:** current source commit で real pre-push を通し、verified PR を `pre` へ merge 後、承認済み promotion、production deploy、same-SHA tag / GitHub Release `v2026.07.29` を完了し、Issue #195 に evidence を記録する。Issue #192 は別の bounded child packets として進める。

### English

- **2026-07-29 00:42:43 JST / Background:** Issue #195. Article-end discovery called `getCollection('entries')` per page and mapped/sorted roughly 1,482 routes to recompute a `count:first:last` cache signature. Although the index maps were reused after the first page, the full-collection signature sort and route-lane work repeated across 2,884 localized entry pages, while middle-entry title, tag, and `related`-link changes did not invalidate the cache.
- **Scope:** The entry-discovery fingerprint and route graph, entry static-path integration, the EntryLayout collection boundary, focused regression tests, architecture/FSD/RTM docs, the trilingual README, release registry, this CHANGELOG, and `releases/v2026.07.29.md`. Wiki facts, `ja`/`en` translation bodies, card markup/CSS/limits, and wikilink/backlink semantics are unchanged.
- **Primary files:** `site/src/lib/entryDiscovery.mjs`, `site/src/pages/[lang]/[...slug].astro`, `site/src/layouts/EntryLayout.astro`, `tools/entry_discovery.test.ts`, `docs/04-architecture/entry-discovery-build-graph.md`, `docs/{README,03-requirements/rtm,04-architecture/astro-site-architecture,05-functional-specs/ui-ux}.md`, `README.md`, and `releases/{README,v2026.07.29}.md`.
- **Steps:** Fixed the design packet in the Issue and a tracked architecture note before implementation. Replaced the old signature with a SHA-256 fingerprint over length-prefixed normalized route/domain/slug/title, sorted tags, ordered `related` targets, and loader body for every entry. Built route-only lanes once from the canonical collection in entry `getStaticPaths()`, then passed each language/route localized payload through static props. Removed the collection load and full-corpus scan from `EntryLayout` while preserving explicit related → backlinks → shared tags → read-next → sibling fallback and the 5/3/5 caps.
- **Validation:** The focused cache/parity suite passed 4 tests / 22 assertions; the full repository suite passed 86 tests / 294 assertions; Astro check passed across 44 files with 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints; and the duplicate-ID audit reported zero issues across 2,969 pages. Under the same warmed content-cache / empty-`dist` conditions, build time fell from 51.21 seconds to 21.18 seconds, a 30.03-second or 58.6% reduction, while the static-route phase fell from 37.42 seconds to 7.78 seconds, a 79.2% reduction. All 2,884 before/after entry-discovery fragments were byte-identical with zero differences. Full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` also passed every gate, including 20 release-doc files / 18 strict notes / 76 grandfathered notes; 1,489 wiki entries / zero issues / zero canonical drift; 1,482 generated API entries / fixed-timestamp byte identity / zero leakage; 1,442 current mirrors per language; zero production vulnerabilities; 2,968 Pagefind pages; assembly of 6,258 Astro files plus 3,077 raw files; all 13 required routes; 5,864 final HTML routes; 6,041 unique generated internal URLs; and `git diff --check`.
- **Known notes:** The cache is process-local and creates no persistent build artifact. The backlink index is built once per build process from resolved wikilinks. The `.opinions/**` public boundary remains the human decision in Issue #193 and is untouched.
- **Follow-up:** Pass the real pre-push hook on the current source commit, merge the verified PR into `pre`, then complete the approved promotion, production deploy, same-SHA tag/GitHub Release `v2026.07.29`, and Issue #195 evidence. Continue Issue #192 through separate bounded child packets.

## 2026-07-28 - Release-state reconciliation and catch-up promotion preparation (#188)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-28 23:48:51 JST / 背景:** Issue #188。公開 `main` は `e89c0a588b2fb73357256ee9cd5f2681574fdb36` の 2026-07-01 snapshot に留まり、検証済み `pre` は `06399b4a1781bd21c420ccacf7b4e24f219778f5` まで 47 commits 進んでいた。一方で repository release note は 91 件、Git tag と GitHub Release は各 70 件で、22 staging notes、欠落していた `v2026.05.29` repository note、2026-07-01 に root CHANGELOG から外れた旧詳細 timeline の状態が明文化されていなかった。
- **範囲:** release note / tag / GitHub Release の状態照合、旧 CHANGELOG timeline の immutable archive、`pre → main` catch-up Release `v2026.07.28-9`、三言語 README、本 CHANGELOG、release / deployment runbook、root homepage と AI / crawler discovery surface。wiki corpus の facts、`.opinions/**` policy、truthfulness content queue は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `releases/README.md`、`releases/v2026.05.29.md`、`releases/v2026.07.28-9.md`、`README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、`docs/08-operations/release-process.md`、`docs/08-operations/deployment-runbook.md`、`index.html`、生成 discovery files。
- **実行手順:** 全 note / tag / GitHub Release を basename で照合し、同名 tag / Release がある公開済み note、個別には発行しない 22 staging records、current catch-up Release を一つの三言語台帳に分類した。`v2026.05.29` note は既存 public Release body を要約して復元した。cutover 直前の 6,127 行 CHANGELOG は repository に複製せず、exact parent commit `528c39e81be2faf3e7d17c1197da9a5569985e51` の [immutable archive](https://github.com/jasonhnd/finwiki/blob/528c39e81be2faf3e7d17c1197da9a5569985e51/CHANGELOG.md) へ接続した。運用フローを work branch → verified PR to `pre` → human-approved promotion PR to `main` → production deploy → same-SHA tag / GitHub Release に統一し、direct `main` push を禁止した。
- **検証結果:** 初期照合で Git tag と GitHub Release の 70 versions は完全一致し、tag / Release にだけ存在した version は `v2026.05.29` のみ、note にだけ存在した version は意図的 staging 22 件のみであることを確認した。full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` は 82 tests / 272 assertions、Astro check 44 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints、Astro 2,969 pages、entry metadata 2,884 / issues 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,076 raw public files、13 required routes、final HTML audit 5,864 routes、generated URL audit 6,040 unique URLs、production vulnerability 0 で PASS。release docs は 19 files / 17 strict notes / 76 grandfathered notes、wiki audit は 1,489 entries / issues 0 / canonical drift 0、generated-surface drift は API 1,482 entries / fixed-timestamp byte identity / leakage 0 を確認した。現 branch の real pre-push と PR Required Verification / Dependency Audit / Vercel は current HEAD で green を確認してから promotion し、その URL を Issue evidence に残す。
- **既知の注意点:** `Required verification` workflow はこの catch-up promotion で初めて `main` に到達するため、workflow が存在しない旧 `main` に先行して required check を設定すると promotion を deadlock させる。bootstrap は approved promotion と successful deploy の後に main protection を即時有効化する順序を取る。ignored local delivery configuration は repository authority とせず、tracked runbooks と branch protection を authority とする。
- **残タスク:** release-prep PR を `pre` へ merge し、exact `pre` boundary を `main` へ人手承認 promotion する。Deploy FinWiki と representative routes を確認後、同じ main merge SHA に tag / GitHub Release `v2026.07.28-9` を作成する。その後 `main` に PR requirement、current `Required verification`、admin enforcement、force-push / deletion prohibition を設定し、Issues #184 / #188 に closeout evidence を記録する。Issue #193 の `.opinions/**` binary policy choice は別途 human decision を待つ。

- **验证结果:** 初始对账确认 70 个 Git-tag version 与 70 个 GitHub-Release version 完全一致；tag / Release 独有的 version 只有 `v2026.05.29`，note 独有的 version 只有有意保留的 22 份 staging record。完整 `bun run verify --out _site` 以 82 tests / 272 assertions、Astro check 44 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints、Astro 2,969 pages、entry metadata 2,884 / issues 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,076 raw public files、13 required routes、final HTML audit 5,864 routes、generated URL audit 6,040 unique URLs、production vulnerability 0 通过。release docs 为 19 files / 17 strict notes / 76 grandfathered notes；wiki audit 为 1,489 entries / issues 0 / canonical drift 0；generated-surface drift 确认 API 1,482 entries / fixed-timestamp byte identity / leakage 0。promotion 还要求 current HEAD 的 real pre-push 与 PR Required Verification / Dependency Audit / Vercel 全部为 green，并把 URL 保留为 Issue evidence。
- **已知注意事项:** `Required verification` workflow 将通过本次 catch-up promotion 首次进入 `main`；如果提前在尚无该 workflow 的旧 `main` 上要求此 check，会造成 promotion deadlock。因此 bootstrap 顺序是在 approved promotion 与 successful deploy 后立即启用 main protection。ignored local delivery configuration 不作为 repository authority；tracked runbook 与 branch protection 才是权威。
- **后续事项:** 将 release-prep PR merge 到 `pre`，再对 exact `pre` boundary 进行 human-approved promotion to `main`。Deploy FinWiki 与 representative routes 验证通过后，在同一个 main merge SHA 上创建 tag / GitHub Release `v2026.07.28-9`。随后为 `main` 设置 PR requirement、current `Required verification`、admin enforcement、禁止 force-push / deletion，并把 closeout evidence 写入 Issues #184 / #188。Issue #193 的 `.opinions/**` binary policy choice 仍单独等待 human decision。

- **検証結果:** focused suite は 16 tests / 59 assertions PASS。parity test と `bun run i18n:status` はともに 1,442 source entries を確認し、ja / en は各 current 1,442、stale / orphaned / missing 0、fidelity ok 1,442。`--force --dry-run` は write-free で 1,442 jobs を発見した。future-domain fixture は directory list 変更なしで新 entry を発見し、更新済み smoke target は 56 / 46 masks を生成した。full `bun run verify --out _site` も 82 tests / 272 assertions、Astro check 44 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints、Astro 2,969 pages、entry metadata 2,884 / issues 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,072 raw files、13 required routes、final HTML audit 5,864 routes、generated URL audit 6,036 unique URLs、production vulnerability 0 で PASS した。
- **既知の注意点:** discovery denominator の修復であり、自動翻訳 API を呼ばず、既存 mirror を書き換えない。manifest-driven prep は canonical corpus 外の stale path を安全に無視する。
- **残タスク:** full canonical gate、real pre-push、remote HEAD、PR checks を確認し、Issue #182 に closeout evidence を記録して maintainer review を待つ。self-close はしない。

- **2026-07-28 15:21:09 JST / 背景:** Issue #198。#185 で実 Astro typecheck を導入した後、check 自体は green だったが、Node global `process` の型不足 1 件、deprecated Zod `.passthrough()` 2 件、未使用 helper 2 件の計 5 hints が残っていた。
- **範囲:** `site/package.json` / `site/bun.lock`、`site/scripts/translate.mjs`、`site/src/content.config.ts`、wikilink / responsive-table plugin、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.28-6.md`、release generator が同期する public surface。wiki entry、`ja` / `en` mirror、translation algorithm、table / wikilink runtime behavior は変更しない。

- **実行手順:** exact dev dependency `@types/node@26.1.2` と file-level Node type reference を追加した。2 collection schema は deprecated chain を等価な `z.looseObject(...)` に置換し、unknown frontmatter key を引き続き保持する。reference が存在しない `kanaCount` と HAST `hasMeaningfulFirstColumn` だけを削除した。
- **検証結果:** `bun run verify --out _site` の full canonical gate は PASS。frozen install は 276 installs / 377 packages を変更なしで再現し、site production audit は vulnerability 0。Astro check は 39 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 0 hints、root test は 50 tests / 174 assertions、Astro build は 2,969 pages / duplicate ID 0、Pagefind は 2,968 pages、assembly は 6,258 Astro files + 3,065 raw files、13 required routes、assembled route audit は 6,029 unique internal URLs で全て PASS。strict release check、generated-surface drift、`git diff --check` も PASS。release-inclusive snapshot は Markdown 1,579、public pages 1,578、sitemap URLs 1,482、domains 40、audited entries 1,489。
- **既知の注意点:** `@types/node` は dev-only type surface で runtime bundle を変更しない。`z.looseObject` は従来の passthrough behavior を維持し、実 corpus / mirror 全体の build がその互換性を確認した。
- **残タスク:** real pre-push、remote HEAD、PR checks を確認する。Issue #198 に closeout evidence を記録して maintainer review を待ち、self-merge / self-close はしない。

## 2026-07-28 - Scheduled truthfulness history and aggregation repair (#191)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-28 15:02:38 JST / 背景:** Issue #191。weekly truthfulness workflow は毎回 `fact_freshness_queue_growth=monitor` / `no historical artifact` を固定出力し、既に成功済みの scheduled artifacts を読まなかった。実 artifact は 2026-07-06 以降 49 → 415 → 433 → 458 actionable rows を持つが、two-cycle growth threshold は一度も評価されなかった。factual consistency の repeated group 集計は top-level `row.path` のみを見ていたため、実 row の `left.path` / `right.path` が別 source でも 0 になった。さらに current provenance JSON は約 2.6 MiB まで増え、Bun 1.3.14 synchronous pipe が 768 KiB で stdout を truncate して `audit:all` が half JSON parse error で停止した。
- **範囲:** `tools/audit_runner.ts`、focused test、49 → 415 → 417 historical summary fixtures、`.github/workflows/truthfulness-audit.yml`、NFR / RTM / recurring cadence / toolchain / test plan / truthfulness runbook、三言語 README、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.28-5.md`、および release generator が同期する public surface。wiki entry、translation mirror、audit detection heuristic、release hard gate は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `tools/audit_runner.ts`、`tools/audit_runner.test.ts`、`tools/fixtures/audit-history/**`、`.github/workflows/truthfulness-audit.yml`、`docs/03-requirements/nfr.md`、`rtm.md`、`docs/04-architecture/recurring-audit-cadence.md`、`docs/06-implementation/toolchain.md`、`docs/07-quality/test-plan.md`、`docs/08-operations/truthfulness-audit-runbook.md`、`README.md`、本 CHANGELOG、release note。
- **実行手順:** `--history-dir` を追加し、prior summary を再帰読込して日付で dedupe / sort し、current より前の最新 2 件と current count だけを summary trend に残す。連続 2 delta がともに正のとき growth を advisory `tripped` とし、history 不足は `monitor`、2 回目が非増加なら `not_tripped` とした。repeated group は row / left / right の unique paths を集計する。workflow は read-only `actions: read` で最新 2 件の成功 scheduled artifact を `$RUNNER_TEMP` へ download する。child audit stdout は private temporary file に capture し、完全読込後に cleanup して pipe cap を回避する。summary schema には bounded dates/counts だけを追加し、local path は追加しない。
- **検証結果:** focused suite は 8 tests / 21 assertions PASS。fixture は 49 → 415 → 417 と +366 / +2 を再現して growth `tripped`、negative/reset と insufficient-history cases も PASS。real repository の historical `audit:all --as-of 2026-07-14` は current actionable 416、49 → 415 → 416、growth `tripped`、repeated groups 8 で完走した。current 2.6 MiB provenance JSON と synthetic 120,000 rows を truncate せず parse し、workflow YAML parser も PASS。post-source-commit release regeneration に対する full `bun run verify --out _site` は 58 tests / 195 assertions、Astro check 39 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 hints、Astro 2,969 pages、duplicate ID 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,065 raw files、13 required routes、6,029 unique internal URLs、production vulnerability 0 で PASS した。release-inclusive snapshot は Markdown 1,579、public pages 1,578、sitemap URLs 1,482、domains 40、audited entries 1,489。
- **既知の注意点:** 2026-07-20 / 2026-07-27 の live scheduled artifacts は issue 起票時の fixture 後に 433 / 458 へ増えている。fixture は acceptance の observed 49 → 415 → 417 regression contract を固定し、production workflow は各 run で直近 2 successful scheduled artifacts を使う。threshold は advisory のままで自動 issue / edit / hard failure を行わない。
- **残タスク:** real pre-push、push、remote HEAD、PR checks を確認する。Issue #191 に closeout evidence を記録して maintainer review を待ち、self-merge / self-close はしない。

### English

- **2026-07-28 15:02:38 JST / Background:** Issue #191. The weekly truthfulness workflow hard-coded `fact_freshness_queue_growth=monitor` and `no historical artifact` on every run instead of reading successful scheduled artifacts. Real artifacts carried 49 → 415 → 433 → 458 actionable rows from 2026-07-06 onward, but the two-cycle growth threshold was never evaluated. Repeated factual-consistency groups inspected only top-level `row.path`, returning zero even when a real row had different `left.path` and `right.path` sources. Current provenance JSON also grew to about 2.6 MiB, while the Bun 1.3.14 synchronous pipe truncated stdout at 768 KiB and made `audit:all` fail while parsing half a JSON document.
- **Scope:** `tools/audit_runner.ts`, focused tests, 49 → 415 → 417 historical summary fixtures, `.github/workflows/truthfulness-audit.yml`, NFR/RTM/recurring-cadence/toolchain/test-plan/truthfulness-runbook docs, trilingual README, this CHANGELOG, `releases/v2026.07.28-5.md`, and public surfaces synchronized by the release generator. Wiki entries, translation mirrors, audit detection heuristics, and the release hard gate are unchanged.
- **Primary files:** `tools/audit_runner.ts`, `tools/audit_runner.test.ts`, `tools/fixtures/audit-history/**`, `.github/workflows/truthfulness-audit.yml`, `docs/03-requirements/nfr.md`, `rtm.md`, `docs/04-architecture/recurring-audit-cadence.md`, `docs/06-implementation/toolchain.md`, `docs/07-quality/test-plan.md`, `docs/08-operations/truthfulness-audit-runbook.md`, `README.md`, this CHANGELOG, and the release note.
- **Steps:** Added `--history-dir`, recursively loaded prior summaries, deduplicated/sorted them by date, and kept only the latest two points before the current run plus the current count in the summary trend. Growth becomes advisory `tripped` when both consecutive deltas are positive, remains `monitor` with insufficient history, and becomes `not_tripped` when the second transition does not grow. Repeated groups now aggregate unique row/left/right paths. The workflow downloads the latest two successful scheduled artifacts into `$RUNNER_TEMP` with read-only `actions: read`. Child audit stdout is captured in a private temporary file, read completely, and cleaned up to bypass the pipe cap. The summary schema adds bounded dates/counts only and no local paths.
- **Validation:** The focused suite passed with 8 tests / 21 assertions. The fixture reproduced 49 → 415 → 417 and +366 / +2 with growth `tripped`; negative/reset and insufficient-history cases also passed. Historical `audit:all --as-of 2026-07-14` completed against the real repository at current actionable 416, trend 49 → 415 → 416, growth `tripped`, and eight repeated groups. The current 2.6 MiB provenance JSON and a synthetic 120,000-row payload parsed without truncation, and the workflow YAML parser passed. Full `bun run verify --out _site` against the post-source-commit release regeneration passed with 58 tests / 195 assertions, Astro check across 39 files with 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 hints, 2,969 Astro pages, zero duplicate IDs, 2,968 Pagefind pages, 6,258 Astro files plus 3,065 raw files in the assembled artifact, all 13 required routes, 6,029 unique internal URLs, and zero production vulnerabilities. The release-inclusive snapshot contains 1,579 Markdown files, 1,578 public pages, 1,482 sitemap URLs, 40 domains, and 1,489 audited entries.
- **Known notes:** Live scheduled artifacts on 2026-07-20 and 2026-07-27 grew to 433 and 458 after the issue-time fixture. The fixture locks the accepted observed 49 → 415 → 417 regression contract, while production uses the latest two successful scheduled artifacts on each run. Thresholds remain advisory with no automatic issue, edit, or hard failure.
- **Follow-up:** Confirm the real pre-push, push, remote HEAD, and PR checks. Attach closeout evidence to Issue #191, wait for maintainer review, and do not self-merge or self-close.

## 2026-07-28 - Current JST review-overdue boundary (#194)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-28 14:25:07 JST / 背景:** Issue #194。entry evidence strip の `review_by` warning は `2026-06-02` と直接比較していたため、repository と deployment が時間経過しても overdue boundary が更新されず、実際の build 日付と矛盾していた。
- **範囲:** entry layout の overdue 判定、shared JST date helper、focused date-boundary tests、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.28-3.md`。wiki entry の事実本文、frontmatter、`ja` / `en` translation mirror、domain map は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `site/src/layouts/EntryLayout.astro`、`site/src/lib/reviewDate.ts`、`tools/review_date.test.ts`、`CHANGELOG.md`、`releases/v2026.07.28-3.md`。
- **実行手順:** `Intl.DateTimeFormat` と明示的な `Asia/Tokyo` time zone から static build 時点の `YYYY-MM-DD` を組み立てる pure helper を追加した。`review_by` が今日より前の場合のみ overdue とし、今日 / 将来 / 欠落 / 不正値は warning にしない。UTC 14:59:59 と 15:00:00 の JST calendar rollover、および昨日 / 今日 / 明日の境界を unit tests に固定し、`EntryLayout.astro` から hard-coded date を除去した。
- **検証結果:** focused review-date suite は 4 tests / 10 assertions PASS。full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` は全 54 tests / 184 assertions、Astro check 40 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 既知 hints、Astro 2,969 pages、duplicate HTML id 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,065 raw files、13 required routes、6,029 generated internal URLs、production vulnerabilities 0 を含む全 gate で PASS。`git diff --check` も PASS。
- **既知の注意点:** overdue state は static build の JST 日付で計算され、再 build ごとに更新される。既に deploy 済みの static artifact は次の build まで変化しない。5 Astro hints は Issue #198 の既存 scope。
- **残タスク:** commit 後に history-aware discovery date を再同期し、real pre-push を通す。push 後に `pre` 向け PR を作成し、CI / preview evidence を Issue #194 に記録する。`main` と GitHub Release は承認済み promotion まで変更しない。

## 2026-07-28 - Entry HTML metadata and route-level language alternates (#180)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-28 14:10:58 JST / 背景:** Issue #180。`llms.txt` は各 entry HTML head に schema.org `Article` JSON-LD、frontmatter 由来の `finwiki:*` meta、per-entry JSON API / raw Markdown alternate があると案内していたが、実際の shared layout はこれらを出力していなかった。また `ja` / `en` hreflang は entry route ではなく language homepage を指し、machine surface の説明と rendered HTML が一致していなかった。
- **範囲:** entry metadata helper、shared base / entry layout、route-preserving canonical / hreflang、API / Markdown resource alternate、build 後の全 entry metadata audit、canonical verification wiring、focused tests、三言語 README、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.28-2.md`。wiki entry の事実本文、`ja` / `en` mirror 本文、domain map、公開情報の判断は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `site/src/lib/entryHead.ts`、`site/src/layouts/Base.astro`、`site/src/layouts/EntryLayout.astro`、`tools/entry_metadata_audit.ts`、`tools/entry_head.test.ts`、`tools/entry_metadata_audit.test.ts`、`tools/verify.ts` と test、`package.json`、`README.md`、`CHANGELOG.md`、`releases/v2026.07.28-2.md`。
- **実行手順:** source frontmatter の scalar / list / structured values を lossless に正規化し、各 localized entry route から self-canonical、同一 domain / slug の `ja` / `en` alternate、日本語 route の `x-default`、API JSON、raw Markdown、Article JSON-LD、8 系統の `finwiki:*` meta を組み立てる shared contract を実装した。JSON-LD は markup-sensitive content を安全に serialize し、meta attribute は Astro の escaping を維持する。
- **回帰防止:** `html:metadata` は API index から 1,442 entry route を列挙し、`ja` / `en` の 2,884 built pages を監査する。canonical、hreflang、resource alternate、frontmatter meta、Article URL / language / headline / main entity、keywords / aliases / citations、additionalProperty consistency のいずれかが欠落・不一致なら fail closed し、canonical verification の build 後 gate として実行する。
- **検証結果:** `bun run verify --out _site` の full canonical gate は 56 tests / 190 assertions、Astro check 40 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 hints、dependency vulnerabilities 0、Astro 2,969 pages、entry metadata 2,884 pages / issues 0、duplicate ID 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,065 raw files、13 required routes で PASS。generated route audit も 6,029 unique internal URLs を確認し、release docs、strict release、docs、surface drift、i18n 1,442 current mirrors / language、index count、wiki link、`git diff --check` を含む全 gate が PASS した。
- **既知の注意点 / 残タスク:** source / release / discovery surface 同期と full canonical verification は完了した。real pre-push、commit、push、remote verification、PR / CI / Vercel はまだ完了していない。pass evidence を Issue #180 と `pre` 向け PR に記録し、maintainer review を待つ。self-merge / self-close は行わず、`main` publish と GitHub Release は承認済み promotion 時だけ行う。

### English

- **2026-07-28 14:10:58 JST / Background:** Issue #180. `llms.txt` told consumers that each entry HTML head exposed schema.org `Article` JSON-LD, frontmatter-backed `finwiki:*` metadata, and per-entry JSON API/raw Markdown alternates, but the shared rendered layout emitted none of them. Its `ja` / `en` hreflang links also pointed to language homepages instead of the current entry route, leaving the machine-surface description inconsistent with the rendered HTML.
- **Scope:** Entry metadata helper, shared base/entry layouts, route-preserving canonical/hreflang links, API/Markdown resource alternates, a post-build audit of every entry, canonical-verification wiring, focused tests, the trilingual README, this CHANGELOG, and `releases/v2026.07.28-2.md`. Factual wiki-entry bodies, `ja` / `en` mirror bodies, the domain map, and public-information judgments are unchanged.
- **Primary files:** `site/src/lib/entryHead.ts`, `site/src/layouts/Base.astro`, `site/src/layouts/EntryLayout.astro`, `tools/entry_metadata_audit.ts`, `tools/entry_head.test.ts`, `tools/entry_metadata_audit.test.ts`, `tools/verify.ts` and its test, `package.json`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, and `releases/v2026.07.28-2.md`.
- **Steps:** Implemented a shared contract that losslessly normalizes scalar, list, and structured source-frontmatter values, then derives a self-canonical, same-domain/slug `ja` and `en` alternates, Japanese `x-default`, API JSON, raw Markdown, Article JSON-LD, and eight `finwiki:*` metadata families for every localized entry route. JSON-LD safely serializes markup-sensitive content while Astro retains correct attribute escaping.
- **Regression protection:** `html:metadata` enumerates 1,442 entry routes from the API index and audits their 2,884 built `ja` / `en` pages. It fails closed on any missing or inconsistent canonical, hreflang, resource alternate, frontmatter meta, Article URL/language/headline/main entity, keywords/aliases/citations, or additional-property contract, and now runs as a post-build canonical-verification gate.
- **Validation:** The full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` gate passed with 56 tests / 190 assertions, Astro check across 40 files with 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 hints, zero dependency vulnerabilities, 2,969 Astro pages, 2,884 entry-metadata pages with zero issues, zero duplicate IDs, 2,968 Pagefind pages, assembly of 6,258 Astro files plus 3,065 raw files, and all 13 required routes. ## 2026-07-28 - Final HTML internal-route crawl and broken-link repair (#183)

### 日本語

- **実行手順:** Bun `HTMLRewriter` で final artifact の全 `[href]` を source HTML の public URL から解決する gate を追加した。同一 origin の HTTP(S) target は query / fragment を除き、shared checker で exact case、output boundary、non-symlink、non-empty regular file を検証する。diagnostic は source file、tag、original href、resolved URL、failure reason を出力する。JapanFG card を既存の `/ja/domains/megabanks/` へ修正し、Binance Japan raw path を wikilink 化、隣接する wikilink / provenance marker と説明括弧の間に空白を入れて CommonMark の誤 link 化を解消した。生成された wiki audit report が local worktree basename を domain として公開しないよう、frontmatter domain のない root entry は安定した `root` に分類した。

## 2026-07-28 - Ordered translation protection and failed-output quarantine (#181)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-28 13:31:14 JST / 背景:** Issue #181。翻訳保護の `verify()` は placeholder を sort してから比較していたため、同じ placeholder 集合が残っていれば順序を入れ替えても通過した。また、direct / staged translation pipeline は検証失敗後も `fidelity: needs_review` の formal mirror を書き込み、Astro site は badge を付けるだけでその本文と title を通常 page、一覧、count、localized wikilink に使用していた。
- **範囲:** placeholder / Markdown URL masking、順序を含む verification diagnostics、direct / staged translation の atomic commit boundary、ignored local review queue、formal mirror の publishability filter、localized title lookup、entry / browse / domain / home rendering guard、focused regression tests、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.28.md`。既存 source corpus と `site/src/content/i18n/{ja,en}/` mirror 本文は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `site/scripts/protect.mjs`、`site/scripts/translation-output.mjs`、`site/scripts/translate.mjs`、`site/scripts/commit-translate.mjs`、`site/scripts/protect.test.mjs`、`site/src/lib/translations.ts`、`site/src/lib/siteIndex.mjs`、`site/src/layouts/EntryLayout.astro`、`site/src/pages/**`、`CHANGELOG.md`、`releases/v2026.07.28.md`。
- **実行手順:** placeholder を出現順の完全な sequence として比較し、reorder、duplicate、missing、unknown token を個別に診断する。wikilink に加えて inline / reference / autolink / bare Markdown URL も mask し、数字を含む URL 全体を unmask まで保護する。検証失敗時は既存 mirror を一切変更せず、masked source、candidate、verification metadata、mask values を Git ignored の `site/.cache/translation-review/` に隔離する。direct pipeline は title / body を一つの atomic mirror update として扱う。site loader、title index、translation count は `fidelity: ok` のみを使用し、layout にも fail-closed guard を置いた。
- **検証結果:** `bun test site/scripts/protect.test.mjs` は 12 tests / 47 assertions PASS。reorder、duplicate、missing、unknown、Markdown URL、既存 mirror 非上書き、review artifact、publishability の negative cases を確認した。root `bun test` は 54 tests / 197 assertions PASS。`cd site && bun run check` は 41 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 既知 hints、`bun run i18n:status` は ja / en 各 1,442 current、stale / orphaned / missing 0、fidelity ok 1,442、`cd site && bun run build` は 2,969 pages で PASS。
- **既知の注意点:** review queue は operator review 用の local ignored artifact であり、corpus、AI discovery、API、site、publish artifact には入らない。現在の committed mirror に `needs_review` は 0 件であるため、公開 page の内容差分は発生しない。Astro check の 5 hints は Issue #198 の既存 scope。
- **残タスク:** release-document audit と full canonical `bun run verify` を通し、commit / push / PR / CI evidence を Issue #181 に記録する。`pre` への merge、Issue close、`main` promotion、GitHub Release は maintainer review 後に行う。

- **2026-07-28 13:31:14 JST / Background:** Issue #181. Translation `verify()` sorted placeholders before comparison, so a translation could reorder protected facts while retaining the same placeholder set and still pass. The direct and staged pipelines also wrote a formal `fidelity: needs_review` mirror after failed verification, while the Astro site merely displayed a badge and continued using that body and title in normal pages, listings, counts, and localized wikilinks.
- **Scope:** Placeholder and Markdown-URL masking, order-sensitive verification diagnostics, atomic commit boundaries for direct/staged translation, an ignored local review queue, formal-mirror publishability filtering, localized-title lookup, entry/browse/domain/home rendering guards, focused regression tests, this CHANGELOG, and `releases/v2026.07.28.md`. Existing source-corpus and `site/src/content/i18n/{ja,en}/` mirror bodies are unchanged.
- **Primary files:** `site/scripts/protect.mjs`, `site/scripts/translation-output.mjs`, `site/scripts/translate.mjs`, `site/scripts/commit-translate.mjs`, `site/scripts/protect.test.mjs`, `site/src/lib/translations.ts`, `site/src/lib/siteIndex.mjs`, `site/src/layouts/EntryLayout.astro`, `site/src/pages/**`, `CHANGELOG.md`, and `releases/v2026.07.28.md`.
- **Steps:** Compare placeholders as an exact occurrence sequence and report reorder, duplicate, missing, and unknown-token failures separately. In addition to wikilinks, mask inline, reference, autolink, and bare Markdown URLs so an entire URL containing digits remains protected through unmask. On verification failure, leave the existing formal mirror untouched and quarantine the masked source, candidate, verification metadata, and mask values under Git-ignored `site/.cache/translation-review/`. Treat direct-pipeline title/body output as one atomic mirror update. Site loaders, title indexes, and translation counts now accept only `fidelity: ok`, with a defensive fail-closed layout guard.
- **Validation:** `bun test site/scripts/protect.test.mjs` passed 12 tests / 47 assertions, covering reorder, duplicate, missing, unknown, Markdown-URL, existing-mirror preservation, review-artifact, and publishability negative cases. Root `bun test` passed 54 tests / 197 assertions. `cd site && bun run check` passed across 41 files with 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 known hints; `bun run i18n:status` reports 1,442 current mirrors for each of ja/en, zero stale/orphaned/missing, and 1,442 `fidelity: ok`; `cd site && bun run build` passed with 2,969 pages.
- **Known notes:** The review queue is a local ignored operator artifact and cannot enter the corpus, AI discovery, API, site, or publish artifact. The committed mirrors currently contain zero `needs_review` rows, so this change produces no public body-content difference. The five Astro hints remain the existing scope of Issue #198.
- **Follow-up:** Pass release-document auditing and the full canonical `bun run verify`, then record commit/push/PR/CI evidence on Issue #181. Merge into `pre`, close the issue, promote `main`, and create a GitHub Release only after maintainer review.

## 2026-07-27 - AI discovery route and deployed-URL repair (#179)

### 日本語

- **2026-07-27 18:26:55 JST / 背景:** Issue #179。AI discovery surface は一部の Markdown source path を extensionless URL として出力していたが、実際の公開物は日本語 / 英語の localized HTML route、明示的な `.md` raw file、または GitHub fallback であり、source-level audit だけでは deploy 後の 404 や誤った canonical / alternate URL を検出できなかった。`last_modified` は従来から git history を第一 source としていたが、shallow / history-less builder では安定した fallback がなく、filesystem mtime への早すぎる fallback により同一入力・同一 timestamp でも byte drift が起こり得た。
- **範囲:** shared Markdown route resolution、AI discovery generator、API `external_links` normalization、最終 assembled artifact の TXT / structured route audit、generated-surface drift scan、output comparator、canonical verification wiring、full-history workflow checkout、focused route tests、三言語 README の長期運用ルール、本 CHANGELOG、`releases/v2026.07.27-6.md`。wiki entry の事実本文、`ja` / `en` mirror 本文、domain map は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`、`.github/workflows/required-verification.yml`、`lib/markdown_helpers.ts`、`tools/generate_ai_discovery.ts`、`tools/txt_route_audit.ts`、`tools/generated_surface_drift_scan.ts`、`tools/compare_ai_discovery_outputs.ts`、`tools/verify.ts`、`tools/discovery_routes.test.ts`、`README.md`、`CHANGELOG.md`、`releases/v2026.07.27-6.md`。
- **実行手順:** 日本語 HTML canonical を `/ja/{route}/`、英語 alternate を `/en/{route}/`、公開 raw Markdown を明示的な `.md` URL として解決し、static artifact に公開できない source は GitHub URL へ fallback する shared contract を実装した。sitemap、API、AI index を実 deploy URL に揃え、API `external_links` を absolute HTTP(S) に正規化した。TXT、sitemap、AI index、API index、全 API entry の declared internal route URL を最終 assembled output に対して監査し、wrong-origin を fail closed とした。workflow checkout は full history を取得し、shallow / history-less builder では committed discovery date、最後に mtime を使用する。exact drift gate は committed generated surface と、その timestamp を使った一回の clean regeneration を比較する。Git query と shallow-clone fixture は inherited `GIT_*` repository context を除去し、fixture cleanup は suite 完了時に行うため、pre-push と並行 test のどちらでも別 repository を参照・削除しない。
- **検証結果:** focused suite は 24 tests / 102 assertions PASS。`bun run verify --out _site` の full canonical gate も 50 tests / 174 assertions、Astro check 39 files / 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 hints、Astro 2,969 pages、duplicate ID 0、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 Astro files + 3,064 raw files、13 required routes で PASS した。assembled route audit は 6,028 unique internal URLs を確認して PASS し、generated-surface drift scan は committed surface と一回の fixed-timestamp clean regeneration が `last_modified` を含め byte-identical であることを確認した。strict release check も PASS。release-inclusive snapshot は Markdown 1,578、public pages 1,577、sitemap URLs 1,482、domains 40、audited entries 1,489、10,613,259 non-space characters、約 1,703,143 word-like tokens。
- **既知の注意点:** release generator、README / index / discovery surface の同期、full canonical gate は完了した。commit、push、remote verification、PR / CI、Vercel deployment、GitHub Release はまだ実施・確認していない。
- **残タスク:** source commit 後に discovery date を再生成して amend に含める。その後、push、remote HEAD、CI / deployment status を確認し、Issue #179 に最終 evidence を記録する。`main` publish と GitHub Release は dependency review 後の承認済み promotion 時に行う。

### English

- **2026-07-27 18:26:55 JST / Background:** Issue #179. Some AI discovery surfaces emitted extensionless URLs derived from Markdown source paths, while the deployed forms were localized Japanese/English HTML routes, explicit `.md` raw files, or GitHub fallbacks. A source-level audit alone could not detect post-deployment 404s or incorrect canonical/alternate URLs. Git history was already the primary `last_modified` source, but shallow/history-less builders lacked a stable fallback and could reach filesystem mtime too early, allowing identical inputs to drift across regenerations using the same timestamp.
- **Scope:** Shared Markdown route resolution, the AI discovery generator, API `external_links` normalization, TXT and structured route auditing against the final assembled artifact, generated-surface drift scanning, output comparison, canonical-verification wiring, full-history workflow checkout, focused route tests, the trilingual README long-term rule, this CHANGELOG, and `releases/v2026.07.27-6.md`. Factual wiki-entry bodies, `ja` / `en` mirror bodies, and the domain map are unchanged.
- **Primary files:** `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, `.github/workflows/required-verification.yml`, `lib/markdown_helpers.ts`, `tools/generate_ai_discovery.ts`, `tools/txt_route_audit.ts`, `tools/generated_surface_drift_scan.ts`, `tools/compare_ai_discovery_outputs.ts`, `tools/verify.ts`, `tools/discovery_routes.test.ts`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, and `releases/v2026.07.27-6.md`.
- **Steps:** Implemented a shared contract resolving Japanese HTML canonicals to `/ja/{route}/`, English alternates to `/en/{route}/`, and published raw Markdown to explicit `.md` URLs, with GitHub fallback for sources that cannot enter the static artifact. Aligned sitemap, API, and AI indexes with deployed URLs and normalized API `external_links` to absolute HTTP(S). Audited declared internal route URLs from TXT, sitemap, AI index, API index, and every API entry against the final assembled output, failing closed on a wrong origin. Workflow checkouts now fetch full history; shallow/history-less builders fall back to committed discovery dates and use mtime only last. The exact-drift gate compares committed generated surfaces with one clean regeneration using their fixed timestamps. Git queries and the shallow-clone fixture strip inherited `GIT_*` repository context, and fixture cleanup runs after the suite, preventing pre-push and parallel tests from addressing or removing another repository.
- **Validation:** The focused suite passed 24 tests / 102 assertions. The full canonical `bun run verify --out _site` gate also passed with 50 tests / 174 assertions, Astro check across 39 files with 0 errors / 0 warnings / 5 hints, 2,969 Astro pages, zero duplicate IDs, 2,968 Pagefind pages, assembly of 6,258 Astro files plus 3,064 raw files, and all 13 required routes. The assembled route audit passed across 6,028 unique internal URLs, the generated-surface drift scan proved the committed surfaces and one fixed-timestamp clean regeneration byte-identical including `last_modified`, and the strict release check passed. The release-inclusive snapshot contains 1,578 Markdown files, 1,577 public pages, 1,482 sitemap URLs, 40 domains, 1,489 audited entries, 10,613,259 non-space characters, and approximately 1,703,143 word-like tokens.
- **Known notes:** Release generation, README/index/discovery synchronization, and the full canonical gate are complete. Commit, push, remote verification, PR/CI, Vercel deployment, and the GitHub Release have not yet been performed or confirmed.
- **Follow-up:** Regenerate discovery dates after the source commit and include the diff in the amend. Then confirm push, remote HEAD, and CI/deployment status before recording final Issue #179 evidence. Publish `main` and create the GitHub Release only during an approved promotion after dependency review.

### 日本語

- **2026-07-27 17:46:49 JST / 背景:** Issue #178。scheduled truthfulness workflow は repository root の `audit-artifacts/` に `summary.md` を生成していたが、この directory は gitignore、shared Markdown walker、wiki-link walker のいずれにも登録されていなかった。実際に `bun run audit:all --out audit-artifacts` を実行すると、strict release link audit は通常の 1,489 entries / issues 0 から 1,490 entries / issues 1 へ変化して fail した。`summary.json` は repository root と artifact directory、`summary.md` は artifact directory の絶対 local path も記録していた。
- **範囲:** audit runner の default / explicit output boundary、shareable summary schema、GitHub Actions temporary output、shared corpus と wiki-link exclusions、release JSON scan、generated-surface private-path scan、static publisher boundary、focused integration tests、README、NFR / RTM / FSD / toolchain / quality / operations docs、本 CHANGELOG、三言語 release note、root homepage と AI / crawler discovery surface。audit threshold policy と wiki entry の事実本文は変更しない。
- **主要ファイル:** `tools/audit_runner.ts`、`tools/audit_artifact_isolation.test.ts`、`lib/markdown_helpers.ts`、`tools/wiki_link_audit.ts`、`tools/generated_surface_drift_scan.ts`、`tools/assemble_static_publish.ts` と test、`tools/release.ts`、`.gitignore`、`.github/workflows/truthfulness-audit.yml`、`README.md`、関連 `docs/`、`releases/v2026.07.27-5.md` と生成 public snapshot。
- **実行手順:** audit の既定出力を OS temporary directory に維持し、repository 内の明示出力は ignored `audit-artifacts/` またはその子 directory だけに制約した。作成後の real path も再検証し、他の repository path へ向く output を拒否する。child audit は exact current Bun と absolute tool path plus explicit `--root-dir` で起動し、fixture root を正しく監査できるようにした。shareable JSON / Markdown summary から root / artifact path を削除し、CI は `$RUNNER_TEMP` から summary と artifact を公開する。shared walker と independent wiki walker の両方で directory を除外し、surface drift は audit path / common local path を検出、publisher は manifest selection から除外し Astro output に現れた場合も fail する。

- **既知の注意点:** truthfulness threshold は引き続き advisory で、release hard gate には変更しない。CLI stdout は local operator が artifact を見つけるため実際の output directory を表示するが、upload される `summary.json` / `summary.md` には path を保持しない。本 branch は #184 → #185 → #186 → #177 の dependency stack 上にあり、前提が `pre` に入るまでは重複 PR を開かない。
- **次の作業:** canonical verification と real pre-push を完了して branch / evidence を Issue #178 に記録する。dependency merge 後に最新 `pre` へ replay して fresh PR review / CI を待つ。self-merge / self-close はせず、`main` publish と GitHub Release は承認済み promotion 時だけ行う。

### English

- **2026-07-27 17:46:49 JST / Background:** Issue #178. The scheduled truthfulness workflow generated `summary.md` under root `audit-artifacts/`, but that directory was absent from gitignore, the shared Markdown walker, and the independent wiki-link walker. Reproducing `bun run audit:all --out audit-artifacts` changed the strict release link audit from the normal 1,489 entries / zero issues to 1,490 entries / one issue and failed. `summary.json` also recorded the absolute repository root and artifact directory, while `summary.md` recorded the absolute artifact directory.
- **Scope:** Audit-runner default/explicit output boundaries, shareable summary schema, GitHub Actions temporary output, shared corpus and wiki-link exclusions, release JSON scanning, generated-surface private-path scanning, static-publisher boundaries, focused integration tests, README, NFR / RTM / FSD / toolchain / quality / operations docs, this CHANGELOG, trilingual release note, root homepage, and AI/crawler discovery surfaces. - **Primary files:** `tools/audit_runner.ts`, `tools/audit_artifact_isolation.test.ts`, `lib/markdown_helpers.ts`, `tools/wiki_link_audit.ts`, `tools/generated_surface_drift_scan.ts`, `tools/assemble_static_publish.ts` and its tests, `tools/release.ts`, `.gitignore`, `.github/workflows/truthfulness-audit.yml`, `README.md`, related `docs/`, `releases/v2026.07.27-5.md`, and the generated public snapshot.
- **Steps:** Kept default audit output under the OS temporary directory and restricted explicit in-repository output to ignored `audit-artifacts/` or its descendants. Re-check the real path after creation so an output resolving to another repository location is rejected. Child audits now use the exact current Bun, absolute tool paths, and explicit `--root-dir`, allowing a fixture root to be audited correctly. Removed root/artifact paths from shareable JSON and Markdown summaries, and moved CI summary/artifact publication to `$RUNNER_TEMP`. Excluded the directory in both walkers, taught surface drift to detect audit/common-local paths, rejected it from manifest selection, and fail publishing if it appears in Astro output.
- **Validation:** After reproducing the real pre-fix failure, focused publish-boundary and audit-then-regenerate suites passed 10 tests / 64 assertions. The fixture proved fixed-timestamp discovery, API, and sitemap output was byte-identical before and after auditing, with neither the fixture absolute path nor `audit-artifacts` in summaries/public output. With ignored artifacts present in the real repository, strict release preserved the pre-change baseline of 1,576 Markdown files and 1,489 entries / zero issues; surface drift passed with 1,482 API entries and zero docs/audit/local-path leaks. After adding the release record, `bun run verify --out _site` passed at 1,577 Markdown files, Astro check 39 files / 0 errors / 5 hints, 38 tests / 144 assertions across seven files, 2,969 Astro pages, 2,968 Pagefind pages, assembly of 6,258 + 3,063 files, and all 13 required routes.
- **Known notes:** Truthfulness thresholds remain advisory and are not promoted into the hard release gate. CLI stdout still prints the real output directory so a local operator can find it, but uploaded `summary.json` / `summary.md` do not retain that path. This branch is stacked after #184, #185, #186, and #177, so no duplicate PR will be opened until prerequisites enter `pre`.

### 中文

- **2026-07-27 17:46:49 JST / 背景:** Issue #178。scheduled truthfulness workflow 会在仓库根的 `audit-artifacts/` 生成 `summary.md`，但该目录未加入 gitignore、shared Markdown walker 或独立 wiki-link walker。实际执行 `bun run audit:all --out audit-artifacts` 后，strict release link audit 从正常的 1,489 entries / issues 0 变成 1,490 entries / issues 1 并失败；`summary.json` 还记录了 repository root 与 artifact directory 的绝对路径，`summary.md` 也记录了 artifact directory 的绝对路径。
- **范围:** audit runner 的 default / explicit output boundary、shareable summary schema、GitHub Actions temporary output、shared corpus 与 wiki-link exclusion、release JSON scan、generated-surface private-path scan、static publisher boundary、聚焦 integration tests、README、NFR / RTM / FSD / toolchain / quality / operations docs、本 CHANGELOG、三语 release note、根首页与 AI / crawler discovery surface。不改变 audit threshold policy 或 wiki entry 事实正文。

- **执行步骤:** 保持 audit 默认输出到 OS temporary directory，并把 repository 内显式 output 限制在已 ignored 的 `audit-artifacts/` 或其子目录；创建后再次核验 real path，拒绝实际指向其他 repository 位置的 output。child audit 改用 exact current Bun、absolute tool path 与显式 `--root-dir`，使 fixture root 能被正确审计。从 shareable JSON / Markdown summary 中删除 root / artifact path，CI 改从 `$RUNNER_TEMP` 发布 summary 与 artifact。两个 walker 均排除该目录，surface drift 新增 audit/common-local path 检测，publisher 从 manifest selection 中排除，并在 Astro output 出现时直接 fail。
- **验证结果:** 先复现修复前的真实 failure，随后 publish boundary 与 audit-then-regenerate 聚焦测试共 10 tests / 64 assertions 全部通过。fixture 证明 fixed timestamp 的 discovery / API / sitemap 在 audit 前后 byte-identical，summary 与 public output 均不含 fixture absolute path 或 `audit-artifacts`。即使真实 repository 中保留 ignored artifact，strict release 仍保持修复前 baseline 的 1,576 Markdown、1,489 entries / issues 0；surface drift 以 API 1,482 entries、docs / audit / local-path leakage 0 通过。加入 release record 后，`bun run verify --out _site` 以 1,577 Markdown、Astro check 39 files / 0 errors / 5 hints、7 files / 38 tests / 144 assertions、Astro 2,969 pages、Pagefind 2,968 pages、assembly 6,258 + 3,063 files 与 13 required routes 全部 PASS。
- **已知注意事项:** truthfulness threshold 继续保持 advisory，不升级为 hard release gate。CLI stdout 为方便 local operator 定位产物仍显示实际 output directory，但上传的 `summary.json` / `summary.md` 不保存该路径。本 branch 叠加在 #184、#185、#186 与 #177 dependency stack 之后，前置项进入 `pre` 前不会创建重复 PR。
